
FUNCTION 'QCM 8005 - Hourly DHU' GLOBAL
    IF (WebAutomation.IfWebPageContains.WebPageContainsElement BrowserInstance: Browser Control: appmask['Web Page \'h ... yDHUByLine\'']['Div \'/QCM8005 - Hourly DHU by Line\'']) THEN
        WebAutomation.GoToWebPage.GoToWebPage BrowserInstance: Browser Url: Data8005 WaitForPageToLoadTimeout: 60
        LOOP FOREACH Current_QCM_Hourly_Checkpoint IN QCM_8005_Hourly_Checkpoints
            SET SubflowRetryCount TO 0
            SET MaxSubflowRetries TO 2
            SET DownloadSuccess TO $'''False'''
            LOOP WHILE SubflowRetryCount < MaxSubflowRetries AND DownloadSuccess = $'''False'''
                WAIT (WebAutomation.WaitForWebPageContent.WebPageToContainElement BrowserInstance: Browser Control: appmask['Web Page \'h ... yDHUByLine\'']['Input text \'dtp_8005\'']) FOR 30
                ON ERROR WaitForWebPageContentError
                    SET SubflowRetryCount TO SubflowRetryCount + 1
                    WebAutomation.GoToWebPage.GoToWebPage BrowserInstance: Browser Url: Data8005 WaitForPageToLoadTimeout: 60
                    GOTO 'Retry Checkpoint 8005'
                ON ERROR TimeoutError
                    SET SubflowRetryCount TO SubflowRetryCount + 1
                    WebAutomation.GoToWebPage.GoToWebPage BrowserInstance: Browser Url: Data8005 WaitForPageToLoadTimeout: 60
                    GOTO 'Retry Checkpoint 8005'
                END
                @@RepairWithAIVision: 'True'
WebAutomation.PopulateTextField.PopulateTextFieldUsePhysicalKeyboard BrowserInstance: Browser Control: appmask['Web Page \'h ... yDHUByLine\'']['Input text \'dtp_8005\''] Text: FormattedDateTime Mode: WebAutomation.PopulateTextMode.Replace UnfocusAfterPopulate: True WaitForPageToLoadTimeout: 60
                ON ERROR REPEAT 10 TIMES WAIT 3
                END
                WAIT (WebAutomation.WaitForWebPageContent.WebPageToContainElement BrowserInstance: Browser Control: appmask['Web Page \'h ... yDHUByLine\'']['Div \'SEWING ENDLINE QCQC Checkpoint *\'']) FOR 30
                ON ERROR WaitForWebPageContentError
                    SET SubflowRetryCount TO SubflowRetryCount + 1
                    WebAutomation.GoToWebPage.GoToWebPage BrowserInstance: Browser Url: Data8005 WaitForPageToLoadTimeout: 60
                    GOTO 'Retry Checkpoint 8005'
                ON ERROR TimeoutError
                    SET SubflowRetryCount TO SubflowRetryCount + 1
                    WebAutomation.GoToWebPage.GoToWebPage BrowserInstance: Browser Url: Data8005 WaitForPageToLoadTimeout: 60
                    GOTO 'Retry Checkpoint 8005'
                END
                @@RepairWithAIVision: 'True'
WebAutomation.PopulateTextField.PopulateTextFieldUsePhysicalKeyboard BrowserInstance: Browser Control: appmask['Web Page \'h ... yDHUByLine\'']['Input text \'FIN TOP SIDE QC\''] Text: Current_QCM_Hourly_Checkpoint Mode: WebAutomation.PopulateTextMode.Replace UnfocusAfterPopulate: True WaitForPageToLoadTimeout: 60
                ON ERROR REPEAT 10 TIMES WAIT 3
                END
                WAIT 2
                WebAutomation.PressButton.PressButton BrowserInstance: Browser Control: appmask['Web Page \'h ... yDHUByLine\'']['Button \'VIEW\''] WaitForPageToLoadTimeout: 60
                ON ERROR PressButtonError
                    SET SubflowRetryCount TO SubflowRetryCount + 1
                    WebAutomation.GoToWebPage.GoToWebPage BrowserInstance: Browser Url: Data8005 WaitForPageToLoadTimeout: 60
                    GOTO 'Retry Checkpoint 8005'
                END
                LABEL 'Wait for tool ber 1 QCM 8005'
                WAIT (WebAutomation.WaitForWebPageContent.WebPageToContainElement BrowserInstance: Browser Control: appmask['Web Page \'h ... yDHUByLine\'']['Span \'viewer_toolbar_export\'']) FOR 60
                ON ERROR WaitForWebPageContentError
                    SET SubflowRetryCount TO SubflowRetryCount + 1
                    WebAutomation.GoToWebPage.GoToWebPage BrowserInstance: Browser Url: Data8005 WaitForPageToLoadTimeout: 60
                    GOTO 'Retry Checkpoint 8005'
                ON ERROR TimeoutError
                    SET SubflowRetryCount TO SubflowRetryCount + 1
                    WebAutomation.GoToWebPage.GoToWebPage BrowserInstance: Browser Url: Data8005 WaitForPageToLoadTimeout: 60
                    GOTO 'Retry Checkpoint 8005'
                END
                WAIT 5
                WebAutomation.PressButton.PressButton BrowserInstance: Browser Control: appmask['Web Page \'h ... yDHUByLine\'']['Span \'viewer_toolbar_export\''] WaitForPageToLoadTimeout: 60
                WAIT (WebAutomation.WaitForWebPageContent.WebPageToContainElement BrowserInstance: Browser Control: appmask['Web Page \'h ... yDHUByLine\'']['List item \'Excel Export\'']) FOR 2 
                ON ERROR REPEAT 5 TIMES WAIT 2
                ON ERROR WaitForWebPageContentError
                    GOTO 'Wait for tool ber 1 QCM 8005'
                ON ERROR TimeoutError
                    GOTO 'Wait for tool ber 1 QCM 8005'
                END
                WebAutomation.PressButton.PressButton BrowserInstance: Browser Control: appmask['Web Page \'h ... yDHUByLine\'']['List item \'Excel Export\''] WaitForPageToLoadTimeout: 60
                WAIT (UIAutomation.WaitForWindowContent.WindowToContainElement Element: appmask['Window \'QCM8005 - Hourly DHU by Line - Google Chrome\'']['Window \'Save As\'']) FOR 300 
                ON ERROR WaitForWindowContentError
                    SET SubflowRetryCount TO SubflowRetryCount + 1
                    WebAutomation.GoToWebPage.GoToWebPage BrowserInstance: Browser Url: Data8005 WaitForPageToLoadTimeout: 60
                    GOTO 'Retry Checkpoint 8005'
                ON ERROR TimeoutError
                    SET SubflowRetryCount TO SubflowRetryCount + 1
                    WebAutomation.GoToWebPage.GoToWebPage BrowserInstance: Browser Url: Data8005 WaitForPageToLoadTimeout: 60
                    GOTO 'Retry Checkpoint 8005'
                END
                UIAutomation.PopulateTextField.PopulateTextField TextField: appmask['Window \'QCM8005 - Hourly DHU by Line - Google Chrome\'']['Edit \'File name:\''] Text: $'''%Download_Path%\\%CurrentFactory%-%FormattedDateTimeForNaming%-DHU By Line Hourly for 8005 (%Current_QCM_Hourly_Checkpoint%).xlsx''' Mode: UIAutomation.PopulateTextMode.Replace ClickType: UIAutomation.PopulateMouseClickType.SingleClick
                UIAutomation.PressButton Button: appmask['Window \'QCM8005 - Hourly DHU by Line - Google Chrome\'']['Button \'Save\''] Simulate: False
                ON ERROR PressButtonError
                    SET SubflowRetryCount TO SubflowRetryCount + 1
                    WebAutomation.GoToWebPage.GoToWebPage BrowserInstance: Browser Url: Data8005 WaitForPageToLoadTimeout: 60
                    GOTO 'Retry Checkpoint 8005'
                END
                WAIT (File.WaitForFile.Created File: $'''%Download_Path%\\%CurrentFactory%-%FormattedDateTimeForNaming%-DHU By Line Hourly for 8005 (%Current_QCM_Hourly_Checkpoint%).xlsx''') FOR 120 
                ON ERROR TimeoutError
                    GOTO 'Wait for tool ber 1 QCM 8005'
                END
                SET DownloadSuccess TO $'''True'''
                LABEL 'Retry Checkpoint 8005'
                IF DownloadSuccess = $'''False''' THEN
                    WAIT 3
                END
            END
            IF (File.IfFile.DoesNotExist File: $'''%Download_Path%\\%CurrentFactory%-%FormattedDateTimeForNaming%-DHU By Line Hourly for 8005 (%Current_QCM_Hourly_Checkpoint%).xlsx''') THEN
                Variables.AddItemToList Item: $'''❌ %CurrentFactory%-%FormattedDateTimeForNaming%-DHU By Line Hourly for 8005 (%Current_QCM_Hourly_Checkpoint%) - Failed after %MaxSubflowRetries% attempts''' List: Missing_Files
            END
        END
    ELSE
        Variables.AddItemToList Item: $'''⚠️ %CurrentFactory% - QCM 8005 - Report page not available''' List: Missing_Files
    END
END FUNCTION
